        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h1>client/v3.0.0-beta</h1>
<p>This is the first beta release of the Milvus Go SDK v3, targeting Milvus 3.0. It is compared against client/v2.6.5.</p>
<blockquote>
<p>This is a beta release. APIs and protocol details may change before v3.0.0 GA.</p>
</blockquote>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li>Migrates the Go module to github.com/milvus-io/milvus/client/v3.</li>
<li>Decouples the standalone client from Milvus server-side pkg modules, substantially reducing server-only transitive dependencies.</li>
<li>Adds collection snapshot management and asynchronous restore workflows.</li>
<li>Adds read-only external collections with manual refresh support.</li>
<li>Adds search aggregation, search by primary-key IDs, query ordering, and namespace-scoped operations.</li>
<li>Adds client-side telemetry and structured RPC error inspection.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Breaking Changes and Migration</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Module path: Update all imports from:</p>
<p>github.com/milvus-io/milvus/client/v2</p>
<p>to:</p>
<p>github.com/milvus-io/milvus/client/v3</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Proto types: Applications directly using generated Milvus messages must migrate from milvus-proto/go-api/v2 to milvus-proto/go-api/v3. Although many high-level builders retain<br>
familiar APIs, v2 and v3 protobuf types are not interchangeable.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Installation:</p>
<p>go get github.com/milvus-io/milvus/client/<a href="mailto:v3@v3.0.0-beta">v3@v3.0.0-beta</a></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Go version: The module’s go directive is Go 1.24.9. The client README recommends Go 1.24.12 or later.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Telemetry behavior: Client telemetry is enabled by default. Set ClientConfig.TelemetryConfig.Enabled to false to disable heartbeat and metrics reporting.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>New Features</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Collection snapshots: Added APIs to create, drop, list, and describe collection-scoped snapshots. Snapshots can be restored asynchronously to new collections, with APIs for querying<br>
and listing restore jobs. Snapshot data can also be pinned and unpinned to control garbage collection. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3410264879" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/44361" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/44361/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/44361">#44361</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4045007617" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/48143" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48143/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48143">#48143</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>External collections: Added schema builders for external data sources and external-field mappings:</p>
<ul>
<li>Schema.WithExternalSource</li>
<li>Schema.WithExternalSpec</li>
<li>Field.WithExternalField</li>
</ul>
<p>External collections can be searched and queried using standard client APIs and refreshed through RefreshExternalCollection, GetRefreshExternalCollectionProgress, and<br>
ListRefreshExternalCollectionJobs. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3787839342" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/46886" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/46886/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/46886">#46886</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3885497473" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/47492" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47492/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47492">#47492</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4390562822" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49531" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49531/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49531">#49531</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Search aggregation: Added bucket aggregation builders supporting multi-field keys, nested aggregations, top hits, ordering, and avg, sum, count, min, and max metrics. Results are<br>
returned through ResultSet.AggregationBuckets. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4631015833" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50448" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50448/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50448">#50448</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Search by primary-key IDs: Added NewSearchByIDsOption and AnnRequest.WithIDs for using existing entities as vector-search inputs. Int64 and VarChar primary keys are supported.<br>
(<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3906217583" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/47633" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47633/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47633">#47633</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Namespace-scoped operations: Added WithNamespace support to Search, Hybrid Search, Query, iterators, Insert, Upsert, and Delete options. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4538900956" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50153" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50153/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50153">#50153</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Query ordering: Added QueryOption.WithOrderByFields, accepting expressions such as price:desc and name:asc. An explicit query limit is required when ordering is used. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4837129038" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/51173" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51173/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51173">#51173</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4845996642" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/51220" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51220/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51220">#51220</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Client telemetry: Added configurable collection of request counts, errors, and latency metrics, including P99 latency, recent-error tracking, periodic server heartbeats, and server-<br>
pushed command handling. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3891147986" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/47523" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47523/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47523">#47523</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3894981013" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/47542" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47542/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47542">#47542</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Structured RPC errors: Added public RPCError, common error sentinels, ErrorCode, and IsRetryableError. RPC errors with matching Milvus codes can now be inspected using errors.Is.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Dynamic StructArray fields: Added Client.AddCollectionStructField and NewAddCollectionStructFieldOption, including validation and preservation of parent nullable and max_capacity<br>
metadata. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4579604998" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50276" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50276/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50276">#50276</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>RBAC descriptions: Added user and role descriptions, role-description updates through AlterRole, and force-drop support for roles. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4547551321" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50184" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50184/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50184">#50184</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4547807636" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50186" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50186/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50186">#50186</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3814014595" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/47071" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47071/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47071">#47071</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Import two-phase commit: Added REST wrappers for committing or aborting bulk-import jobs through CommitImport and AbortImport. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4546120588" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50177" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50177/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50177">#50177</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Message salvage API: Added the high-level Client.DumpMessages streaming wrapper for replication and recovery workflows. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4598712257" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50343" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50343/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50343">#50343</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4673758671" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/50574" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50574/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50574">#50574</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>MinHash: Added FunctionTypeMinHash support to the Go SDK function API. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3590456776" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/45322" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/45322/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/45322">#45322</a>)</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>Improvements and Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Default gRPC keepalive, reconnect, and receive-size options are now always applied before user-provided DialOptions. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4295987033" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49186" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186">#49186</a>)</li>
<li>Row-based Insert and Upsert builders now preserve row-based processing when chaining partition, namespace, partial-update, and Array-operation options.</li>
<li>Client-local error, retry, crypto, concurrency, property, and type helpers replace the dependency on Milvus server packages.</li>
<li>Updated the v3 protobuf, gRPC, protobuf runtime, and supporting Go dependencies.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Known Limitations</h3>
<ul>
<li>This SDK release is intended for Milvus 3.0 beta and is not guaranteed to be compatible with Milvus 2.6 servers.</li>
<li>ExportSnapshot and RestoreExternalSnapshot expose preview API contracts in this release, but the server implementation currently returns Unimplemented.</li>
<li>Flush a collection before creating a snapshot if growing-segment data must be included.</li>
<li>External collections are read-only; Insert, Upsert, Delete, Import, partition management, and schema alteration are not supported.</li>
<li>Primary keys must remain unique across namespaces within the same collection because delete and upsert tombstones remain collection-scoped.</li>
<li>Search aggregation cannot be combined with legacy group-by, offsets, or search iterators. Bracketed JSON-path expressions are not yet supported.</li>
<li>Search by primary-key IDs is not supported in Hybrid Search or Search Iterator.</li>
</ul></div>
</div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v2.6.20</h2>
<p>Release date: July 14, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
<th>Java SDK Version</th>
<th>Go SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>2.6.20</td>
<td>2.6.16</td>
<td>2.6.17</td>
<td>2.6.22</td>
<td>2.6.20</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>We are excited to announce the release of Milvus v2.6.20! This release improves query scheduling and batching, index loading, filtering performance, streaming rebalancing, and observability. It also resolves correctness and reliability issues across JSON filtering, streaming recovery, text indexing, analyzer configuration, and GPU_CAGRA operations.</p>
<h3>Improvements</h3>
<ul>
<li>Added named C++ thread-pool activity metrics and Grafana monitoring (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50299" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50299/hovercard">#50299</a>)</li>
<li>Improved QueryCoord scheduling by decoupling task dispatch from distribution polling to allow independent scheduling intervals (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50774" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50774/hovercard">#50774</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50777" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50777/hovercard">#50777</a>)</li>
<li>Improved QueryNode query batching by increasing the default NQ grouping limits for larger merged query batches (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50859" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50859/hovercard">#50859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50898" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50898/hovercard">#50898</a>)</li>
<li>Improved index-loading resilience by safely completing pending range reads after partial failures (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50937" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50937/hovercard">#50937</a>)</li>
<li>Optimized VARCHAR primary-key population when loading sealed segments (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51063" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51063/hovercard">#51063</a>)</li>
<li>Improved filter execution performance by skipping null-bitmap processing for all-valid results (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51067" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51067/hovercard">#51067</a>)</li>
<li>Improved channel balancing by enabling the channel-level score balancer by default and introducing a safer default threshold for channel-exclusive mode (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51132" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51132/hovercard">#51132</a>)</li>
<li>Improved streaming rebalancing to trigger immediately when the primary resource group configuration changed (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51147" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51147/hovercard">#51147</a>)</li>
<li>Upgraded Knowhere to v2.6.17 to prevent GPU_CAGRA operations from failing with bad_optional_access under the default ef configuration (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51209" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51209/hovercard">#51209</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed incorrect JSON path filter results for missing, null, or type-mismatched values (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50722" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50722/hovercard">#50722</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50723" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50723/hovercard">#50723</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where Marisa string indexes could fail to upload or load when the local temporary directory was missing (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50772" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50772/hovercard">#50772</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where stale streaming writes could retry indefinitely after their target collection or partition was dropped (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50849" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50849/hovercard">#50849</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50895" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50895/hovercard">#50895</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where cluster-level load configuration could override user-specified collection replica settings without force override enabled (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50860" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50860/hovercard">#50860</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where analyzer runtime settings and YAML updates were not applied to the Rust analyzer layer (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50998" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50998/hovercard">#50998</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where StorageV2 text index builds could use incorrect paths for existing segment insert logs (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51002" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51002/hovercard">#51002</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where DumpMessages omitted transaction data messages and produced incomplete exports (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51102" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51102/hovercard">#51102</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where ARRAY containment expressions could return incorrect results for float literals (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51130" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51130/hovercard">#51130</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where filters on missing nested JSON values, failed JSON casts, or out-of-range array elements could return incorrect results instead of UNKNOWN (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51135" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51135/hovercard">#51135</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where streaming recovery could omit physical channels recorded in collection metadata and leave required WAL topics unavailable after startup (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51144" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/51144/hovercard">#51144</a>)</li>
</ul></div>
</div>
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Box-footer">

              <h2 class="v-align-middle m-3 f5">No results found</h2>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v2.6.19</h2>
<p>Release date: July 1, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
<th>Java SDK Version</th>
<th>Go SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>2.6.19</td>
<td>2.6.16</td>
<td>2.6.17</td>
<td>2.6.22</td>
<td>2.6.19</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>We are excited to announce the release of Milvus v2.6.19! This release improves text indexing, JSON handling, GPU runtime compatibility, RBAC metadata, and search result serialization. It also fixes correctness and stability issues across WAL recovery, scalar expressions, nullable fields, ArrayOfVector, group-by search, and DataCoord GC.</p>
<h3>Improvements</h3>
<ul>
<li>Added configurable concurrency for function runner text tokenization (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50115" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50115/hovercard">#50115</a>)</li>
<li>Improved mix compaction by building text indexes inline to avoid slow QueryNode fallback index creation (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50160" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50160/hovercard">#50160</a>)</li>
<li>Upgraded GPU Docker images to CUDA 12.9.1 for Ubuntu 22.04 builds and runtime (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50250" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50250/hovercard">#50250</a>)</li>
<li>Added a configuration option for maximum array capacity (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50265" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50265/hovercard">#50265</a>)</li>
<li>Improved S3 PutObject compatibility with OpenSSL FIPS mode by forcing CRC32C checksums (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50360" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50360/hovercard">#50360</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50477" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50477/hovercard">#50477</a>)</li>
<li>Added RBAC role description support across clients, APIs, and role metadata (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50526" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50526/hovercard">#50526</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50535" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50535/hovercard">#50535</a>)</li>
<li>Improved error handling by standardizing on merr with system and input error classification across Milvus (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50545" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50545/hovercard">#50545</a>)</li>
<li>Optimized null predicate evaluation for sealed chunked fields (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50586" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50586/hovercard">#50586</a>)</li>
<li>Improved JSON field handling by enabling JSON shredding by default (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50706" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50706/hovercard">#50706</a>)</li>
<li>Reduced search result serialization overhead by adding an optional zero-copy path for passing search results (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50713" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50713/hovercard">#50713</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50756" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50756/hovercard">#50756</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed an issue where RBAC grantee identifiers could collide due to truncated ID hashes (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50236" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50236/hovercard">#50236</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where Kafka and RMQ WAL recovery could fail to restore checkpoints with negative sentinel message IDs (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50242" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50242/hovercard">#50242</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where scalar-index-backed expression queries could return incorrect results due to cursor misalignment (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50266" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50266/hovercard">#50266</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where CPU-adapted GPU CAGRA indexes could still require GPU resources during loading (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50385" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50385/hovercard">#50385</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where ST_WITHIN queries on nullable GEOMETRY fields could crash standalone during concurrent schema evolution (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50437" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50437/hovercard">#50437</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where AlterCollection could reject requests due to unchanged collection descriptions (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50502" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50502/hovercard">#50502</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50539" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50539/hovercard">#50539</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where describe_user could return empty ghost role names after repeated grant and revoke operations (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50544" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50544/hovercard">#50544</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where DataCoord garbage collection could incorrectly delete text stats files that already stored full paths (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50599" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50599/hovercard">#50599</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50629" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50629/hovercard">#50629</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where invalid StructArray vector dimensions or element counts could be accepted (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50601" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50601/hovercard">#50601</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where group-by search could return duplicate or mismatched group values when results had tied scores (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50621" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50621/hovercard">#50621</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where queries using nullable fields could return incorrect results after expression rewriting (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50627" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50627/hovercard">#50627</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where importing ArrayOfVector float64 data from Parquet could fail or parse vector values incorrectly (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50635" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50635/hovercard">#50635</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where highlighted search results could become misaligned for nullable or empty string fields (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50637" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50637/hovercard">#50637</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where ArrayOfVector EmbList indexes could be built with insufficient rows or vectors (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50727" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50727/hovercard">#50727</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50765" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50765/hovercard">#50765</a>)</li>
</ul></div>
</div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v2.6.18</h2>
<p>Release date: June 5, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
<th>Java SDK Version</th>
<th>Go SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>2.6.18</td>
<td>2.6.15</td>
<td>2.6.17</td>
<td>2.6.20</td>
<td>2.6.18</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>We are excited to announce the release of Milvus v2.6.18! This release adds element-level search on Struct fields and nullable vector support, improves QueryNode and QueryCoord scheduling and stability under heavy load, and brings HTTP/2 to the Proxy REST server. It also fixes numerous correctness and stability issues across import, schema evolution, indexing, compaction, and metadata handling.</p>
<h3>Features</h3>
<h4>Nullable vector</h4>
<p>Vector fields can now be declared nullable, so you can insert entities whose embedding is missing or not yet generated without filling in a placeholder. NULL vectors take no extra storage and are skipped automatically during search. For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v2.6.18/nullable-and-default.md">Nullable Fields</a>.</p>
<h4>Element-level search on Struct fields</h4>
<p>You can now run vector search on Struct Array fields at the granularity of individual elements instead of the whole row, with each result reporting the matched element's offset within the array. This lets a query retrieve the specific element that best matches rather than scoring the row as a whole. For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v2.6.18/array-of-structs.md#Vector-search-in-a-StructArray-field">Vector search in a StructArray field</a>.</p>
<h3>Improvements</h3>
<ul>
<li>Added support for importing Arrow FixedSizeList data from Parquet into non-nullable array and dense vector fields (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49870" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49870/hovercard">#49870</a>)</li>
<li>Improved QueryNode read-task scheduling and recovery behavior under heavy load with deadline-aware admission, cleanup, grouping, and metrics (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49900" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49900/hovercard">#49900</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49926" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49926/hovercard">#49926</a>)</li>
<li>Added HTTP/2 support for the proxy REST server, including h2c and ALPN-based TLS listeners (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49964" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49964/hovercard">#49964</a>)</li>
<li>Extended Arrow IO thread pool configuration to DataNode to improve compaction and import throughput (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50100" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50100/hovercard">#50100</a>)</li>
<li>Limited QueryNode delegator post-load concurrency to reduce CPU spikes during segment loading (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49769" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49769/hovercard">#49769</a>)</li>
<li>Optimized QueryCoord collection filtering in ChannelDistManager and reduced temporary allocations in distribution lookups (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49927" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49927/hovercard">#49927</a>)</li>
<li>Upgraded the Pulsar Go client to v0.19.0 and replaced pulsarctl admin usage with pulsaradmin (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49948" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49948/hovercard">#49948</a>)</li>
<li>Optimized ReplicaManager locking to reduce cross-collection contention in QueryCoord (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49950" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49950/hovercard">#49950</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49956" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49956/hovercard">#49956</a>)</li>
<li>Improved REST timeout handling to safely discard late handler writes after request timeouts (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50006" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50006/hovercard">#50006</a>)</li>
<li>Optimized garbage collection for dropped segment index files and metadata (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50172" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50172/hovercard">#50172</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed an issue where using unsupported field types as clustering keys could cause node panics (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48263" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48263/hovercard">#48263</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where the stored_index_files_size metric included inactive index files (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49380" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49380/hovercard">#49380</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where preempted writers could skip segment IDs by upgrading Woodpecker to v0.1.13-hotfix (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49670" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49670/hovercard">#49670</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where requery requests were not pinned to the preferred replica (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49830" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49830/hovercard">#49830</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where bulk imports could fail when file readers encountered a premature EOF (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49867" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49867/hovercard">#49867</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where partial updates could drop dynamic field data after schema evolution (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49913" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49913/hovercard">#49913</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where sealed segments could load incorrectly when non-nullable vector field data was missing (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49918" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49918/hovercard">#49918</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where schema reopen could leave stale indexing references and cause indexing failures or crashes (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49935" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49935/hovercard">#49935</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49937" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49937/hovercard">#49937</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where dropping a collection in streaming mode could follow an incorrect cleanup order (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49962" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49962/hovercard">#49962</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where BM25 sparse vector function outputs were incorrectly treated as loadable raw field data (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49975" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49975/hovercard">#49975</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where QueryCoord could build query targets from dropped channel checkpoints (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50026" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50026/hovercard">#50026</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where transient object storage failures could cause delta log writes in sync tasks to fail without retrying (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50030" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50030/hovercard">#50030</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where retried import tasks could leave stale row counts and cause sort compaction failures (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50070" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50070/hovercard">#50070</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where forced segment assignment could be incorrectly limited by the balance batch size (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50152" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50152/hovercard">#50152</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where target-size manual compaction could select ineligible segments (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50159" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50159/hovercard">#50159</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where replicated AlterLoadConfig operations could keep retrying after a channel was dropped and block metadata cleanup (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50162" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50162/hovercard">#50162</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where sliced indexes could load incorrect sidecar files and affect indexed query behavior (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50167" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50167/hovercard">#50167</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where ST_DWITHIN could panic when validating non-POINT WKT input (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50205" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/50205/hovercard">#50205</a>)</li>
</ul></div>
</div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li>Adds Go SDK support for nullable vector columns across dense, binary, sparse, and int8 vector types.</li>
<li>Adds Array field partial update helpers for <code>ARRAY_APPEND</code> and <code>ARRAY_REMOVE</code> in upsert requests.</li>
<li>Validates that vector fields added to existing collections are nullable before sending <code>AddCollectionField</code> requests.</li>
<li>Bumps the Go SDK version constant to <code>2.6.5</code> and updates <code>milvus-proto/go-api/v2</code> to <code>v2.6.17</code>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>New Features</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>Nullable vector columns</strong>: Added nullable column constructors for vector fields: <code>NewNullableColumnFloatVector</code>, <code>NewNullableColumnBinaryVector</code>, <code>NewNullableColumnFloat16Vector</code>, <code>NewNullableColumnBFloat16Vector</code>, <code>NewNullableColumnInt8Vector</code>, and <code>NewNullableColumnSparseFloatVector</code>. These constructors accept compact non-null vector values plus <code>validData</code>, and validate that the values length matches the valid row count. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4458182758" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49848" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848">#49848</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Nullable vector response decoding</strong>: <code>FieldDataColumn</code> now decodes nullable vector field data using <code>ValidData</code> for <code>FloatVector</code>, <code>BinaryVector</code>, <code>Float16Vector</code>, <code>BFloat16Vector</code>, <code>SparseFloatVector</code>, and <code>Int8Vector</code>. Null rows are preserved in the returned column while compact wire-format vector payloads are expanded back to row-aligned columns. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4458182758" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49848" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848">#49848</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Array partial update operations</strong>: Added upsert helpers <code>WithArrayAppend(fieldName)</code>, <code>WithArrayRemove(fieldName)</code>, and <code>WithFieldPartialOp(fieldName, op)</code> for Array field partial updates. Non-<code>REPLACE</code> field ops are serialized into <code>UpsertRequest.FieldOps</code> and automatically enable <code>partial_update</code> for both column-based and row-based upserts. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4321294823" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49328" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49328/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49328">#49328</a>)</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>AddCollectionField vector validation</strong>: <code>AddCollectionField</code> now validates options before sending the RPC and rejects vector fields added to existing collections unless the field is marked <code>nullable=true</code>, preventing invalid schema changes from reaching the server. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4458182758" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49848" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848">#49848</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>All-null sparse vector handling</strong>: Sparse vector column serialization now handles empty value sets by emitting dimension <code>0</code>, allowing all-null nullable sparse vector columns to round-trip without panics or invalid dimension calculations. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4458182758" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49848" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848">#49848</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Nullable column row counts</strong>: Added <code>Column.ValidCount()</code> support so nullable columns can report the number of non-null rows separately from total row count. Struct-array columns return their total length. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4458182758" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49848" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49848">#49848</a>)</p>
</li>
</ul>

</ul></div>

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v2.6.17</h2>
<p>Release date: May 22, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
<th>Java SDK Version</th>
<th>Go SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>2.6.17</td>
<td>2.6.14</td>
<td>2.6.14</td>
<td>2.6.20</td>
<td>2.6.4</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>We are excited to announce the release of Milvus v2.6.17! This release introduces Array field partial update operators, improves load/search isolation, and resolves several stability and query routing issues.</p>
<h3>Improvements</h3>
<ul>
<li>Added <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v2.6.17/upsert-entities.md#Upsert-ARRAY-fields-with-partial-update-operators"><code>ARRAY_APPEND</code> and <code>ARRAY_REMOVE</code> partial update operators</a> for Array fields, exposed through both gRPC and REST upsert APIs (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49328" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49328/hovercard">#49328</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49724" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49724/hovercard">#49724</a>)</li>
<li>Improved load/search isolation by using separate C++ executor pools and converting SegmentLoad and ReopenSegment to async futures with proper context cancellation (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49764" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49764/hovercard">#49764</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed an issue where filter expression templates could not be used with string field predicates (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49703" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49703/hovercard">#49703</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where stale segment distribution updates after delegator close could corrupt query routing (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49727" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49727/hovercard">#49727</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where releasing a collection could leave stale replica state due to premature metadata cleanup in QueryCoord (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49730" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49730/hovercard">#49730</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where transient shard errors caused replicas to be blacklisted, leading to unnecessary query failures and degraded availability (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49740" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49740/hovercard">#49740</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49776" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49776/hovercard">#49776</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed missing GetReplicateConfiguration RPC forwarding in the proxy service (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49810" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49810/hovercard">#49810</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed a potential use-after-free crash when closing the packed writer multiple times or during compaction cleanup paths (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49816" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49816/hovercard">#49816</a>)</li>
</ul></div>
</div>
  <div data-view-component="true" class="Box-footer">
        <div class="tmp-mb-3">

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v2.6.16</h2>
<p>Release date: May 14, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
<th>Java SDK Version</th>
<th>Go SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>2.6.16</td>
<td>2.6.13</td>
<td>2.6.14</td>
<td>2.6.19</td>
<td>2.6.4</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>We are excited to announce the release of Milvus v2.6.16! This release delivers major stability and performance improvements across L0 compaction, streaming node resource isolation, and proxy query failover, along with critical fixes for delete consistency, replica scaling, and rolling upgrade scenarios.</p>
<h3>Improvements</h3>
<ul>
<li>Increased the default L0 compaction deltalog max count from 30 to 1000 to reduce compaction backlog under high-delete workloads (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47214" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47214/hovercard">#47214</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49122" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49122/hovercard">#49122</a>)</li>
<li>Introduced streaming node resource group isolation, allowing replicas to be assigned strictly within their configured resource groups, plus a new RESTful config inspection endpoint (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48632" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48632/hovercard">#48632</a>)</li>
<li>Rewrote sync manager's key lock dispatcher with per-key FIFO queues and semaphore backpressure for non-blocking submission and graceful shutdown (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49101" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49101/hovercard">#49101</a>)</li>
<li>Added fast-fail retry capping and delegator stall detection so proxy queries failover to a healthy QueryNode immediately instead of burning the full backoff budget on a dead node (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49103" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49103/hovercard">#49103</a>)</li>
<li>Allowed simultaneous pchannel increase and cluster/topology changes in replication config validation (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49214" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49214/hovercard">#49214</a>)</li>
<li>Reduced proxy tail latency and memory pressure during traffic storms by fast-failing Enqueue before TSO/ID allocation and using a non-blocking edge-triggered task notifier (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49259" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49259/hovercard">#49259</a>)</li>
<li>Added a <code>$partial_update</code> field to the proxy access log for Upsert requests, exposing both explicit and implicitly promoted partial-update flags (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49361" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49361/hovercard">#49361</a>)</li>
<li>Accelerated <code>TermExpr IN</code> evaluation with a SIMD (AVX2/AVX512) batch filter, significantly improving query performance for <code>IN</code> predicates (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49427" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49427/hovercard">#49427</a>)</li>
<li>Bumped Go SDK to v2.6.4 with full struct-array support (vector sub-fields, EmbeddingList search, schema validation), gRPC authority configuration, and preserved default gRPC dial options when custom DialOptions are provided (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49443" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49443/hovercard">#49443</a>)</li>
<li>Upgraded lz4_flex to 0.11.6 in the Tantivy binding to remediate <a title="CVE-2026-32829" data-hovercard-type="advisory" data-hovercard-url="/advisories/GHSA-vvp9-7p8x-rfvv/hovercard" href="https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-vvp9-7p8x-rfvv">CVE-2026-32829</a> (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49507" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49507/hovercard">#49507</a>)</li>
<li>Bypassed Knowhere search-pool scheduling for vector iterators to reduce per-Next overhead in iterator-heavy group-by search paths (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49547" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49547/hovercard">#49547</a>)</li>
<li>Exposed Arrow IO thread pool capacity as a refreshable paramtable knob to relieve HIGH-pool stalls under heavy storage v2 read load (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49554" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49554/hovercard">#49554</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49561" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49561/hovercard">#49561</a>)</li>
<li>Parallelized text match index loading on QueryNode to speed up segment load for collections with text indexes (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49608" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49608/hovercard">#49608</a>)</li>
<li>Bumped milvus-storage to fix non-contiguous I/O issues when reading row groups (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49613" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49613/hovercard">#49613</a>)</li>
<li>Sealed growing segments under L0 compaction pressure to avoid prolonged write blocking (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49688" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49688/hovercard">#49688</a>)</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Fixed silent delete loss caused by L0 compaction missing target segments due to inherited incorrect positions from imported data, and corrected DmlPosition aggregation in mix/clustering compaction (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47154" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47154/hovercard">#47154</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47187" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/47187/hovercard">#47187</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48910" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48910/hovercard">#48910</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where collections with inverted indexes failed to load due to incorrect handling of sliced index files (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48542" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/48542/hovercard">#48542</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where queries on nullable array fields with bitmap indexes could return incorrect results due to missing null value persistence (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49073" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49073/hovercard">#49073</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where queries using NOT over templated expressions (e.g. <code>not (field in {vals})</code>) returned wrong results or triggered QueryNode assertion failures (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49184" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49184/hovercard">#49184</a>)</li>
<li>Made config writes synchronously visible in the same process and ensured QueryCoord compliance reports Ready only after leaked segments/channels are released, preventing premature node termination during scale-down (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49212" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49212/hovercard">#49212</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where L0 deltas could be incorrectly skipped, causing stale or incorrect query results (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49228" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49228/hovercard">#49228</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an internal error when using IS NULL / IS NOT NULL with ARRAY element access; the expression is now rejected at parse time with a clear validation error (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49244" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49244/hovercard">#49244</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed built-in RBAC privilege groups drifting from defaults by no longer shipping them in milvus.yaml; runtime now falls through to in-code constants when not explicitly overridden (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49276" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49276/hovercard">#49276</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed a collection-wide query outage triggered by replica-count changes; QueryCoord now honors withUnserviceableShards so the proxy can route through serviceable leaders while the new replica is loading (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49305" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49305/hovercard">#49305</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49311" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49311/hovercard">#49311</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed insert starvation that could occur when sync futures were blocked during write buffer eviction (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49331" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49331/hovercard">#49331</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where schema fields like EnableNamespace were silently dropped during DDL broadcasts from rootcoord, causing downstream components to see zero values after any DDL operation (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49364" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49364/hovercard">#49364</a>)</li>
<li>Improved L0 compaction to fast-finish when no matching L1/L2 segments are found (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49376" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49376/hovercard">#49376</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed expression rewriter to honor the disabled expression optimization config (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49430" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49430/hovercard">#49430</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where segment reopen tasks could stall in QueryCoord by routing them through the existing load segment dispatch path (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49466" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49466/hovercard">#49466</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where collection alias lookups missed the proxy meta cache, causing unnecessary metadata fetches (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49513" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49513/hovercard">#49513</a>, <a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49548" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49548/hovercard">#49548</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed streaming node resource group handling during rolling upgrades so replicas remain correctly assigned when old and new nodes coexist (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49552" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49552/hovercard">#49552</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed a memory leak that occurred when search requests failed validation after placeholder parsing succeeded (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49612" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49612/hovercard">#49612</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed an issue where node shutdown could hang indefinitely when WAL release was blocked (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49625" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49625/hovercard">#49625</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed query visibility issues during rolling upgrades for streaming resource groups (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49629" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49629/hovercard">#49629</a>)</li>
<li>Improved WAL recovery to fail fast on timetick append errors instead of hanging (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49636" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49636/hovercard">#49636</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed JSON stats index build failure when binlogs were missing (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49673" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49673/hovercard">#49673</a>)</li>
<li>Fixed vector index version resolution during rolling upgrades when QueryNodes did not report MaximumIndexVersion (<a href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49675" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49675/hovercard">#49675</a>)</li>
</ul></div>
</div>

        <div class="tmp-mb-3">

        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h2>v3.0-beta</h2>
<p>Release date: May 9, 2026</p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Milvus Version</th>
<th>Python SDK Version</th>
<th>Node.js SDK Version</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>3.0-beta</td>
<td>3.0.0</td>
<td>3.0.0</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>Milvus 3.0-beta extends the Milvus vector database with new integration into the open lake ecosystem: External Collection lets Milvus query external lake tables zero-copy, and Spark can read Milvus collections directly through Snapshot. The release also brings richer retrieval, more expressive schema, deeper text search customization, finer data and model lifecycle controls, and more operator-side controls. Milvus 3.0 is the core kernel of Zilliz Lakebase, powering its unified serving, discovery, and batch.</p>
<h3>Key Features</h3>
<h4>External Collection</h4>
<p>In typical AI data pipelines, terabytes of embeddings and metadata already sit on object storage as Parquet, Lance, or Iceberg tables. Copying that data into Milvus doubles storage cost, adds an ETL pipeline that has to be kept in sync, and shifts data governance away from the customer.</p>
<p>External Collection removes the copy. A Milvus Collection can reference files where they already live, and Milvus manages only the schema, indexes, and query execution. An incremental refresh keeps the Collection aligned with the underlying files. Customers whose data cannot leave the lake, such as finance and healthcare teams, can run vector retrieval against that data where it sits. A single lake-resident dataset can also be served from multiple Milvus instances at once.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/create-an-external-collection.md">Create an External Collection</a>.</p>
<h4>Snapshot</h4>
<p>Serving and batch discovery often need the same Collection at the same time. A/B model evaluation, large-scale deduplication, backfill validation, and version rollback all need a stable view of the Collection while writes are still going in.</p>
<p>Snapshot creates a point-in-time, read-only view of a Collection by referencing existing segments instead of copying data, so the marginal storage cost is close to zero. Batch jobs can read from the Snapshot under MVCC-style isolation while the live Collection keeps accepting writes.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/snapshots.md">Snapshots</a>, <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/manage-snapshots.md">Manage Snapshots</a>, and <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/snapshot-use-cases.md">Snapshot Use Cases</a>.</p>
<h4>Query / Search Order By</h4>
<p>Search and Query now accept multi-field ordering, with the sort pushed down into the Milvus kernel and <code>ASC</code> / <code>DESC</code> settable per field. This closes a common production gap: Top-K by distance alone often does not match the business need when the most similar item is not the cheapest, the most recent, or the most popular.</p>
<p>Applications no longer have to over-fetch results and re-sort on the client to express composite ranking.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/single-vector-search.md#Sort-Search-Results-by-Scalar-Fields--Milvus-30x">Sort Search Results by Scalar Fields</a> and <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/get-and-scalar-query.md#Sort-Query-Results--Milvus-30x">Sort Query Results</a>.</p>
<h4>Query Aggregation</h4>
<p>Producing tenant-distribution stats, field-completeness counts, or version-rollout progress from a Milvus Collection used to require pulling matching entities back to the client and aggregating them there. Milvus 3.0 pushes SQL-style scalar aggregation into the kernel. A query call accepts <code>group_by_fields</code> and aggregation expressions in <code>output_fields</code>, including <code>count(*)</code>, <code>count(&lt;field&gt;)</code>, <code>sum(&lt;field&gt;)</code>, <code>avg(&lt;field&gt;)</code>, <code>min(&lt;field&gt;)</code>, and <code>max(&lt;field&gt;)</code>. Aggregation is evaluated server-side after filtering.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/get-and-scalar-query.md#Aggregate-Query-Results--Milvus-30x">Aggregate Query Results</a>.</p>
<h4>Null Vector</h4>
<p>Embeddings are often produced asynchronously, so an entity can arrive before its vector does. Multimodal data has natural gaps too, such as a video without captions or a product without an image. Earlier versions had no good answer: applications either delayed the write until the vector was ready or filled in a placeholder vector, and both choices hurt retrieval quality.</p>
<p>Milvus 3.0 supports NULL on vector fields across all six vector types. Search skips NULL vectors automatically, retrieval quality is unaffected, and NULL vectors take effectively no storage. <code>AddField</code> also extends to vector fields under this change: with <code>nullable=True</code>, an existing Collection can grow new vector fields online without a rebuild.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/nullable-and-default.md">Nullable Fields</a>.</p>
<h4>Custom Dictionary &amp; Synonym Dictionary</h4>
<p>Out-of-the-box tokenizers do not always meet production search quality requirements. Chinese, vertical domains such as medicine, law, and chemistry, and multilingual corpora can benefit substantially from custom dictionaries and synonym tables. Until now, these resources mostly lived as application-side query rewrites.</p>
<p>Milvus 3.0 adds a FileResource mechanism for registering custom tokenizer dictionaries, synonym lists, stop-word lists, and decompounder rules. Once registered, a resource can be referenced from any tokenizer or filter and takes effect on BM25, analyzers, and Text Match. Dictionaries and synonyms can now be versioned and managed centrally instead of scattered across application code.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/manage-file-resources.md">Manage File Resources</a>.</p>
<h4>Entity TTL</h4>
<p>Collection-level and partition-level TTL are too coarse for many lifecycle and compliance scenarios. Different tenants inside the same Collection often have different retention rules, and individual entities may need to expire on a schedule that does not match the rest of the Collection.</p>
<p>Milvus 3.0 supports per-entity TTL. Declare a <code>TIMESTAMPTZ</code> field in the schema, mark it as the TTL field through a Collection property, and Milvus reclaims expired entities automatically. This covers right-to-be-forgotten requests, expiring session data, and bounded conversation history without application-side cleanup.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/set-collection-ttl.md#Set-entity-level-TTL--Milvus-30x">Set Entity-level TTL</a>.</p>
<h4>MinHash DIDO (Doc-in, Doc-out)</h4>
<p>Milvus 2.6 added the <code>MINHASH_LSH</code> index for set-based near-duplicate detection, but applications still had to compute MinHash signatures before writing data into Milvus.</p>
<p>Milvus 3.0 adds a server-side MinHash function. Declare a <code>VARCHAR</code> input field and a <code>BINARY_VECTOR</code> output field in the schema, attach a <code>FunctionType.MINHASH</code> function, and Milvus computes the signatures during insert, bulk insert, and search. Together with <code>MINHASH_LSH</code>, this supports deduplication workflows for large datasets, fingerprinting, and plagiarism detection inside Milvus.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/minhash-function.md">MinHash Function</a>.</p>
<h4>EmbList + DISKANN</h4>
<p>The "one entity = one vector" assumption no longer fits modern retrieval. Long documents get split into many chunks, late-interaction models such as ColBERT emit one vector per token, and multimodal entities can carry several views.</p>
<p>EmbList stores a variable-length vector list per entity, with <code>DISKANN</code> as the on-disk index. The disk path keeps RAM usage under control when the corpus exceeds memory budgets. EmbList + <code>DISKANN</code> is the first variant of the broader StructList family in this RC. The rest of the family, including StructList filtering and Muvera / Lemur multi-vector acceleration, is targeted for the official 3.0 release.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/search-with-embedding-lists.md">Search with Embedding Lists</a>.</p>
<h4>Force Merge</h4>
<p>Production workloads accumulate segment fragmentation over time, which causes query-latency jitter and inflated storage.</p>
<p>Milvus 3.0 adds the ability to trigger segment compaction explicitly during off-peak windows, in both synchronous and asynchronous modes.</p>
<p>For more information, refer to <a href="/milvus-io/milvus/blob/v3.0-beta/force-merge.md">Force Merge Compaction</a>.</p>
<h4>Storage V3</h4>
<p>Milvus 3.0 introduces Storage V3, a manifest-based columnar storage engine where data and metadata live on S3-compatible object storage. Each dataset version is captured as an immutable manifest snapshot, an Avro-encoded file that records which column groups, delta logs, and statistics comprise the dataset.</p>
<p>Manifests are compact Avro files, and delta logs record entity-level deletes without rewriting data files. This keeps metadata overhead small as datasets grow. The manifest also decouples metadata tracking from the query path, allowing a Collection to manage more segments without degrading query performance.</p>
<p>Because states are stored on object storage, the dataset is self-descriptive: any reader with access to the storage path can discover and interpret it without a central catalog. This property underpins External Collection, Snapshot, and future lake integrations.</p></div>
</div>

          <a class="Link--inTextBlock" href="https://docs.github.com/github/authenticating-to-github/displaying-verification-statuses-for-all-of-your-commits">Learn about vigilant mode</a>.
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
</details>

        </div>

</div>
  </div>
        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li>Completes Go SDK (<code>client/v2</code>) struct-array support, especially vector sub-fields and EmbeddingList/MAX_SIM search.</li>
<li>Fixes custom gRPC <code>DialOptions</code> so they no longer drop the SDK's default keepalive, backoff, and max receive message-size settings.</li>
</ul>
<h3>New Features</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>Struct-array vector sub-field columns</strong>: Added vector-array column types for struct-array vector sub-fields: <code>ColumnFloatVectorArray</code>, <code>ColumnFloat16VectorArray</code>, <code>ColumnBFloat16VectorArray</code>, <code>ColumnBinaryVectorArray</code>, and <code>ColumnInt8VectorArray</code>. These support <code>ArrayOfVector</code> sub-fields inside struct arrays. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>EmbeddingList search vector types</strong>: Added <code>entity.FloatVectorArray</code>, <code>entity.Float16VectorArray</code>, <code>entity.BFloat16VectorArray</code>, <code>entity.BinaryVectorArray</code>, and <code>entity.Int8VectorArray</code>, dispatching them to the matching <code>PlaceholderType_EmbList*</code> values. This enables MAX_SIM / EmbeddingList search against struct-array vector sub-fields such as <code>clips[clip_emb]</code>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Struct-array insert/upsert helper</strong>: Added <code>WithStructArrayColumn(name, structSchema, rows)</code> to column-based insert/upsert options. The helper accepts <code>[]map[string]any</code> row data and infers scalar/vector sub-column types from the provided struct schema. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>gRPC authority configuration</strong>: Added <code>ClientConfig.WithGrpcAuthority(authority)</code> for proxy-based routing through the gRPC <code>:authority</code> header. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4295987033" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49186" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186">#49186</a>)</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>Bug Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>Default gRPC dial options are preserved</strong>: <code>Client.dialOptions()</code> now always applies <code>DefaultGrpcOpts</code> before user-provided <code>DialOptions</code>, retaining SDK defaults for keepalive, backoff, and max receive message size while still allowing caller overrides. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4295987033" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49186" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49186">#49186</a>, issue <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4222330528" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/48828" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/issues/48828/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/48828">#48828</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Struct-array schema validation</strong>: Added <code>Schema.Validate()</code> and <code>StructSchema.Validate()</code> and wired validation into <code>CreateCollection</code>. Invalid struct-array sub-fields are rejected before the RPC, including nested array/struct fields, sparse vector sub-fields, duplicate sub-field names, and top-level-only flags such as primary key, partition key, clustering key, nullable, default value, dynamic, and autoID. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>, issue <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293576702" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49163" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49163/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49163">#49163</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Struct-array schema round trip</strong>: <code>Schema.ReadProto()</code> now restores <code>StructArrayFields</code>, and <code>ProtoMessage()</code> preserves <code>max_capacity</code> by propagating the parent setting to sub-fields when needed. <code>Array</code> / <code>ArrayOfVector</code> wrappers from the server are unwrapped back to user-facing scalar/vector sub-field types. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Struct-array write correctness</strong>: <code>columnStructArray</code> now enforces equal lengths across sub-fields, appends a row atomically with rollback on partial sub-field failure, and serializes top-level struct-array field data as <code>DataType_ArrayOfStruct</code>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Vector-array response decoding</strong>: Query/search result decoding now handles <code>DataType_ArrayOfVector</code>, validates nil vector rows, validates payload lengths against dimension/width, rejects invalid binary vector dimensions, and preserves vector-array sub-field data in Go column types. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Builder error handling</strong>: <code>WithStructArrayColumn</code> stores construction errors and returns them from <code>InsertRequest</code> / <code>UpsertRequest</code> instead of panicking during chained builder calls. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4293590514" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/issues/49164" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164/hovercard" href="https://github.com/milvus-io/milvus/pull/49164">#49164</a>)</p>
</li>
</ul>
